# Pi sweep review — CastCircle

Exhaustive per-file pass: 145 code files across 79 batches — model ds4/GLM-5.3-Flash-Q2:off — 2026-08-29.

## Findings

- [medium] lib/data/database/app_database.dart:130-142 — `_step` treats any error message containing 'already exists' as benign already-applied and returns without rethrowing — a real failure whose message merely mentions 'already exists' (e.g. a UNIQUE constraint or table-exists error from a different cause) is silently swallowed, Drift advances user_version, and the missing column/index is never repaired, so every later query dies with "no such column" forever — match on the specific duplicate-column/already-applied error class (or verify the column actually exists before returning) instead of substring matching the message.
- [medium] lib/data/database/app_database.dart:264-270 — `insertRecording` deletes prior rows only when both `productionId` and `scriptLineId` are present in the companion; if either is absent the delete is skipped and `insertOrReplace` on a random `id` primary key piles up duplicate rows for the same (production, line) — the one-per-line invariant documented at lines 255-261 is silently broken and `watchRecordingsForProduction`/`getRecordingsForProduction` return stale duplicates — require both fields present (or fail the insert) before entering the transaction, and delete by the composite key unconditionally.
- [medium] lib/data/database/app_database.dart:276-282 — `markRecordingUploaded` writes `remoteUrl` with `update(...).write(...)` but never checks the returned row count; if no row matches (e.g. the recording was deleted or ids differ) the upload is silently marked as done by the caller while the DB still has `remoteUrl == null` — return/propagate the affected-row count (or throw when 0) so callers can detect the no-op.
- [low] lib/data/database/app_database.dart:316-333 — `_openConnection` runs `PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL` etc. inside `setup` on every lazy open; if the DB file is on a filesystem that doesn't support WAL (some Android storage), the pragma silently fails and the connection continues in journal mode with synchronous=NORMAL, which is unsafe for that mode — check the returned journal mode (or catch/log the pragma result) and fall back to `synchronous=FULL` when WAL is unavailable.
- [low] lib/data/models/cast_member_model.dart:10-13 — `CastRole.fromString` degrades unknown role strings to `CastRole.primary` instead of throwing or returning a distinct unknown role — a malformed/newer-version cloud row silently gains organizer-level semantics downstream wherever `role` is used to gate permissions (assumption: callers treat `primary` as a normal member; if any caller checks `role == CastRole.organizer` for elevated rights, a bad row is silently promoted) — return a nullable/unknown sentinel or log-and-default to `understudy` (the least-privileged role) so the failure direction is safe.
- [low] lib/data/models/cast_member_model.dart:48-69 — `copyWith` uses `??` for every field, so a caller cannot clear optional fields (`userId`, `contactInfo`, `invitedAt`, `joinedAt`) back to null; passing `null` keeps the old value — if any caller relies on copyWith to unset `joinedAt`/`userId` (e.g. "leave production"), the field silently stays set — use a sentinel pattern (`Value<T>`-style or `Object?` marker) for nullable fields, or document that clearing is unsupported.

## Coverage
lib/data/database/app_database.dart — findings: 5
lib/data/models/cast_member_model.dart — findings: 2
- [medium] lib/data/models/production_models.dart:29-31 — copyWith cannot clear nullable fields (scriptPath, joinCode) — passing null keeps the old value, so a "clear script" or "clear join code" update silently retains the previous PDF path / join code and callers cannot express removal — use a sentinel (e.g. `bool clearScriptPath`) or wrap nullable params in `Optional<T>` so `null` means "clear".
- [medium] lib/data/models/rehearsal_models.dart:31 — `duration` returns a negative Duration when `endedAt` precedes `startedAt` (e.g. clock skew or a session ended before it started) — downstream analytics computing average session length get negative values silently — clamp or assert `endedAt.isAfter(startedAt)` at construction.
- [low] lib/data/models/rehearsal_models.dart:33-34 — `completionRate` treats `totalLines == 0` as 0.0 completion rather than an error/undefined state — a rehearsal created with no lines reports 0% instead of being flagged, hiding data-quality problems in analytics — consider returning null or logging when totalLines is 0.
- [low] lib/data/models/rehearsal_models.dart:38 — `struggledLines` filters on `bestScore < 0.7` with the threshold hardcoded — the same threshold is likely used elsewhere (production code / tests) as a named constant; a literal here means a threshold change in one place silently desyncs the other — extract a shared `struggleThreshold` constant (assumption: threshold is duplicated elsewhere; not visible in this batch).
- [info] lib/data/models/production_models.dart:19 — `locale` defaults to `'en-US'` in the constructor rather than being required — a caller that forgets to pass the organizer's chosen locale silently records STT in en-US, mis-transcribing non-English rehearsals with no error — make it required or resolve the default from an explicit app-level locale setting.

## Coverage
lib/data/models/production_models.dart — findings: 2
lib/data/models/rehearsal_models.dart — findings: 3
- [medium] lib/data/repositories/production_repository.dart:244-245 — `markRecordingUploaded` forwards `remoteUrl` straight to the DB update with no validation that it is an https URL or belongs to the production's storage bucket — an authenticated user who can invoke this path (assumption: a caller passes user-influenced URLs, since the repository does no sanitizing itself) can persist arbitrary strings as the recording's remote URL, which later playback/download code will treat as a trusted Supabase storage URL — validate the URL scheme/host (e.g. require https and the expected storage host prefix) before delegating to `_db.markRecordingUploaded`.
- [medium] lib/data/repositories/production_repository.dart:42-61 — `deleteProduction` deletes recording files best-effort but never deletes the recordings' DB rows before `_db.deleteProduction(id)`; if the DB layer lacks a cascade (not visible here), rows referencing deleted files remain — verify the DB cascade exists or delete recording rows explicitly in the same transaction as the production row.
- [low] lib/data/repositories/production_repository.dart:46-52 — file deletion swallows all exceptions (`catch (_)`), so a permission error or wrong path is silently ignored and the caller believes cleanup succeeded — log the failure via the debug log service instead of an empty catch.
- [low] lib/data/repositories/production_repository.dart:56-60 — the five cascade deletes plus the production delete are not wrapped in a transaction; a crash mid-sequence leaves orphaned cast/script/scene rows with the production gone — wrap the cascade in `_db.transaction`.
- [low] lib/data/models/voice_preset.dart:148 — `VoicePresets.byId` silently falls back to `modernAmerican` for any unknown id, so a typo in a preset id resolves to a different production style with no signal — return null (or throw) and let callers handle the miss, or at least log the unknown id.
- [low] lib/data/models/voice_preset.dart:60-131 — several preset voice lists reference voice ids (`af_alloy`, `af_aoede`, `af_kore`, `af_nicole`, `af_river`, `af_sky`, `am_echo`, `am_fenrir`, `am_liam`, `am_puck`) that appear in `voiceLabels` but never in any preset pool, while preset pools use only a subset; if `voiceLabels` is meant to enumerate all selectable voices this is fine, but if a preset pool is expected to cover every labeled voice the map and pools are inconsistent — confirm intent; if pools must be exhaustive, add the missing ids or trim the map.

## Coverage
lib/data/models/voice_preset.dart — findings: 2
lib/data/repositories/production_repository.dart — findings: 4
- [medium] lib/data/services/audio_level_service.dart:36-64 — `volumeFor` caches a fallback 1.0 result on any channel/analysis failure, so a transient `MissingPluginException` or decode error permanently pins the path to unnormalized volume until `invalidate`/`clear` is called — recordings that should be attenuated play at full loudness with no retry — fix by not writing to `_gainCache` when the analysis path threw or returned no usable RMS (only cache successful measurements).
- [medium] lib/data/services/audio_level_service.dart:60-62 — LRU eviction removes `keys.first` from insertion-ordered map, but every successful re-invocation re-inserts the key at the end only when it misses cache; a hot path that hits cache never refreshes its position, so the oldest *inserted* (not least-recently-used) entry is dropped — with ≥512 distinct paths the cache can thrash while the most-recently-used entries are evicted — fix by deleting and re-inserting the key on a cache hit to refresh recency, or use a true LRU structure.
- [low] lib/data/services/audio_level_service.dart:68-70 — `prefetch` fires `volumeFor(path)` without awaiting or guarding the returned Future; an unhandled async error from the channel invoke (e.g. `PlatformException` not caught by the internal `catch`? — it is caught, but the Future is still un-awaited and its completion is invisible) leaves no error path and no way for callers to know warm-up failed — fix by `unawaited(volumeFor(path))` with explicit intent or returning the Future.
- [info] lib/data/services/audio_level_service.dart:19-20 — hardcoded global channel name `com.lineguide/audio_analysis` is guessable and shared; if another plugin/feature registers the same name the handler could be hijacked — verify uniqueness against the rest of the repo (assumption: no other registration seen in provided context).
- [info] lib/data/services/analytics_service.dart:10-11 — `_analytics` getter silently returns null whenever `firebaseAvailable` is false, so every `log*` call becomes a no-op with no logging/metric of the drop; telemetry silently disappears in builds where Firebase is unavailable — consider a debugPrint or counter when dropping events (direction-of-failure: fails toward silent data loss of analytics).

## Coverage
lib/data/services/analytics_service.dart — findings: 1
lib/data/services/audio_level_service.dart — findings: 4
- [medium] lib/data/services/deep_link_service.dart:44-51 — Unvalidated deep link fields beyond the code pattern — `char` and `name` query parameters are accepted from any hostile link after only stripping control/format characters and length-capping; they are later rendered in trusted app chrome (toast/UI), so a crafted link can still inject arbitrary visible text (e.g. a fake "Session expired — re-enter your password" prompt) that no server-side validation ever saw — reachability: unauthenticated internet via a crafted castcircle://join?... link — smallest safe fix: restrict `char`/`name` to a strict allowlist (e.g. `^[A-Za-z0-9 .,'-]{1,60}$`) in `_sanitizeName` and reject the link when they fail, rather than only stripping unsafe chars.
- [medium] lib/data/services/deep_link_service.dart:141-157 — Rejected-link toast fires on every malformed `/join` link with no dedup or rate limit — a hostile app or web page can repeatedly fire the `castcircle://join` intent and spam the user with a 6-second SnackBar each time (UI denial-of-service / phishing surface), and on cold start `rootScaffoldMessengerKey.currentState` may be null so the message silently never shows — reachability: unauthenticated internet — smallest safe fix: debounce the toast (e.g. ignore repeats within N seconds) and log-only when the messenger is unavailable.
- [low] lib/data/services/deep_link_service.dart:113-115 — Bare `catch (e)` around `getInitialLink` swallows all non-timeout errors with only a debugPrint — a real platform error (e.g. plugin misregistration) is silently reduced to "no initial link", so a broken cold-start invite is indistinguishable from a normal launch and never reaches the error log — smallest safe fix: route non-timeout failures through `DebugLogService.instance.logError(LogCategory.general, ...)` like the timeout branch does.
- [low] lib/data/services/deep_link_service.dart:124-128 — Bare `catch (e)` around `uriLinkStream.listen` claims "Invites tapped from now on will do nothing" but only logs — the stream subscription failure is not retried or surfaced to the user, so the whole session's deep-link handling is dead with no recovery path — smallest safe fix: at minimum log via `logError` (already done) and add a retry or user-visible fallback; as written the comment documents a known unrecoverable state with no mitigation.
- [low] lib/data/services/deep_link_service.dart:144-148 — Rejected-link log includes the raw link's scheme/host/path and code length but the code value itself is deliberately omitted — good — however `debugPrint('Deep link received: $uri')` at line 132 prints the full URI including any `char`/`name` payload to the console in debug builds, which can carry user-identifying names — smallest safe fix: log the sanitized `PendingJoin` (or only scheme/host/path) instead of the raw URI.
- [info] lib/data/services/deep_link_service.dart:99 — `latestPendingJoin` is a public mutable field on a global singleton — any caller can read/clear it with no encapsulation; combined with `clearPending()` (161-163) there is no atomicity between "read" and "clear", so two rapid links could interleave — smallest safe fix: make the field private with a getter, or guard read+clear in one synchronized method.
- [low] lib/data/services/contact_picker_service.dart:35-41 — `MissingPluginException` is swallowed and returns null, conflating "platform unsupported" with "user cancelled" — the caller cannot distinguish an unsupported platform from a cancelled pick and may silently treat an error as a normal cancel — smallest safe fix: rethrow `MissingPluginException` (or return a typed result) so callers can branch on unsupported-platform vs cancelled.
- [low] lib/data/services/contact_picker_service.dart:22 — Hard-coded global channel name `'com.tiltastech.castcircle/contacts'` with no owner check — any installed app registering the same channel name on the platform side could intercept; low because the native side is in-repo and the name is namespaced — smallest safe fix: none required if the native handler is in this repo; otherwise add a signature/permission check on the platform side.

- [medium] lib/data/services/demo_production_service.dart:59-93 — `load()` mutates provider state (`currentProductionProvider`, `currentScriptProvider`, `rehearsalCharacterProvider`) and persists to SharedPreferences without any error handling; if `_parseBundledScript()` throws after `existing == null` the production row was already added but the script/character state is never set, leaving a half-initialized demo production that a later call treats as "existing" and reopens with the same broken state — wrap the add in a transaction-like ordering (parse first, then add) or roll back the added production on failure.
- [medium] lib/data/services/demo_production_service.dart:103-113 — `_preselectCharacter` writes the chosen character to SharedPreferences before setting the provider state; if `prefs.setString` succeeds but the notifier write is skipped by an exception (or the app is killed between the two awaits), the stored key says a character was chosen while the in-memory state is stale, so the next launch silently trusts the persisted value — set the notifier state first, then persist, or persist only after the state write succeeds.
- [low] lib/data/services/demo_production_service.dart:109-112 — the fallback "whoever speaks most" sorts a copy of `script.characters` in place via `..sort(...)` on a freshly materialized list; if `script.characters` is a `const`/unmodifiable view (as `ParsedScript.characters` is documented to be immutable in script_models.dart:404-405), `toList()` protects it, but the sort comparator `b.lineCount.compareTo(a.lineCount)` on an empty list would throw on `.first` — guard the `.first` with an isEmpty check since `_parseBundledScript` only guarantees non-empty at parse time, not at this call site.
- [low] lib/data/services/espeak_heteronyms.dart:85-88 — `before.lastIndexOf(word)` returns `-1` when the matched group text differs in case from what `replaceRange` expects after `_matchCase` was applied to the replacement but not to the search; `replaceRange(-1, ...)` throws RangeError on legitimate matches like "Live and let live" where the group captured "Live" but the replacement is built from `_liveVerb` — verify by testing the capitalised imperative case, or search case-insensitively (`before.toLowerCase().lastIndexOf(word.toLowerCase())`).
- [low] lib/data/services/espeak_heteronyms.dart:66-70 — `_matchCase` treats a fully-lowercase original as "no change" and returns the bare replacement, but a title-cased original whose first char equals its uppercase form (e.g. "Live") falls into the second branch and uppercases only the first letter of `liv`, producing "Liv" — acceptable for the imperative rule but inconsistent with the doc comment's promise that capitalisation is copied; confirm intended output or handle the two-letter word explicitly.

## Coverage
lib/data/services/demo_production_service.dart — findings: 3
lib/data/services/espeak_heteronyms.dart — findings: 2
- [medium] lib/data/services/frame_stats_service.dart:37 — jank counter counts frames whose totalSpan exceeds 16.7ms but the report divides by `_buildMs.length` (n) while `_jank` accumulates across the whole window; if `_report` fires when `_buildMs` is empty the division is by zero — guarded by `_buildMs.isNotEmpty` at line 40, but if timings arrive with `totalSpan` populated yet `buildDuration`/`rasterDuration` zero-length lists the counter still inflates — consequence: reported jank percentage can exceed 100% or misreport when the window resets mid-batch — smallest safe fix: count jank per-batch alongside `_buildMs` and reset both together in `_report`.
- [low] lib/data/services/frame_stats_service.dart:46 — `_pct` indexes `sorted[((sorted.length - 1) * p).round()]` without clamping; for p=0.99 on a 1-element list the index is 0 (fine), but for p=1.0 or a rounding edge the index can exceed `sorted.length - 1` — consequence: potential RangeError crash in the perf report path — smallest safe fix: clamp the computed index to `sorted.length - 1`.
- [medium] lib/data/services/kokoro_onnx_service.dart:243-251 — `synthesize` awaits `req.completer.future` and then renames the produced WAV into the cache path; if the completer completes with `null` (stop/died/aborted) the rename is skipped correctly, but when `cachePath` is non-null and `path` is non-null the `renameSync` failure falls back to returning `path` — consequence: the freshly synthesized file stays in tmpDir and is never adopted into the LRU cache, so pruning never sees it and the tmp file leaks per failed rename — smallest safe fix: on rename failure delete the tmp file (or move it into the cache dir with a copy+delete) instead of silently returning the orphan path.
- [medium] lib/data/services/kokoro_onnx_service.dart:213-215 — when the engine is still starting (`_starting != null`) or not started and `_toIsolate == null`, `synthesize` returns `null` immediately for a non-cached line — consequence: a caller awaiting a path gets a silent null with no error surfaced and no retry, so a line that would have been synthesized after startup is dropped for that playback — smallest safe fix: await `_starting` (or trigger `ensureStarted()`) before giving up, or document/return a typed failure so callers can retry.
- [low] lib/data/services/kokoro_onnx_service.dart:203-211 — cache hit path touches mtime via `unawaited(File.setLastModified(...))` before returning; on platforms where setLastModified is unsupported the swallowed error means LRU order silently degrades to creation order — consequence: pruning evicts the wrong entries (FIFO instead of LRU) — smallest safe fix: fall back to rewriting the file's mtime via a touch-write, or log once when the touch fails.
- [low] lib/data/services/kokoro_onnx_service.dart:291-296 — `_schedulePrune` walks `Directory(dir).listSync()` and stats every file inside `Isolate.run`, but `listSync` is called on the closure's isolate while `dir` was captured from the UI isolate — safe, but the walk is unbounded: thousands of WAVs are all stat'd each run — consequence: prune spike on low-end devices every app run once cache exceeds 150MB — smallest safe fix: cap the walk (e.g. bail after N entries) or prune incrementally.
- [low] lib/data/services/kokoro_onnx_service.dart:502-504 — `_writeWav` clamps each sample into int16 range but computes `v` via `(samples[i] * 32767).round()`; NaN or out-of-range float samples from the model produce clamped values silently — consequence: corrupted audio written without any signal — smallest safe fix: assert/log when clamping actually occurs (count clamps and log once).
- [info] lib/data/services/kokoro_onnx_service.dart:57-66 — `voiceIds` hardcodes 28 speaker IDs (0–27) for kokoro-multi-lang-v1_0; if the shipped model pack is a different revision the sid mapping silently misassigns voices — consequence: a requested voice plays as a different speaker with no error — smallest safe fix: verify the mapping against the bundled model's voices.bin at init and log a mismatch.
- [info] lib/data/services/kokoro_onnx_service.dart:50 — `_cancelBelow` is a process-wide ffi-allocated Int32 never freed (documented as intentional at 350-352); confirm this is not leaked per-restart in tests — smallest safe fix: none needed if singleton lifetime is process-wide; verify in test harness.

## Coverage
lib/data/services/frame_stats_service.dart — findings: 2
lib/data/services/kokoro_onnx_service.dart — findings: 7
- [medium] lib/data/services/live_asr_service.dart:111-121 — `_start` awaits `ready.future` with a 30s timeout that resolves to `false`, then calls `stop()` on timeout — but `stop()` increments `_epoch` and kills the isolate while `ensureStarted`'s `_starting` future is still the one being awaited by the caller; the caller gets `false` (correct), yet a concurrent `ensureStarted` after timeout sees `_starting` cleared and may spawn a second isolate while the first is mid-kill — consequence: two recognizer isolates can briefly coexist, doubling native memory on a phone already near OOM (the exact crash the debug log warns about) — fix: guard the timeout path with the same epoch check (`if (epoch != _epoch) return false;`) before calling `stop()`, or set `_starting` before invoking `stop()`.
- [medium] lib/data/services/live_asr_service.dart:139-148 — `stop()` sends `{'cmd': 'dispose'}` then immediately kills the isolate with `Isolate.beforeNextEvent` without awaiting the isolate's `commands.close()`/`return` — consequence: the native recognizer (`recognizer.free()`, `stream.free()` at 238-241) may never run, leaking ONNX model memory each stop/start cycle during a rehearsal session — fix: send dispose, then `await` a short ack (or kill with `Isolate.immediate` after a bounded delay) so the isolate main loop exits before `kill`.
- [medium] lib/data/services/live_asr_service.dart:127-135 — `startUtterance`/`feedPcm`/`endUtterance` silently no-op when `_toIsolate` is null (e.g. between `stop()` and a re-`ensureStarted`, or while `_starting` is pending) — consequence: the rehearsal screen's PCM chunks and utterance boundaries are dropped with no error surfaced, so the partial transcript silently loses words and the user sees a truncated line with no signal that capture was lost — fix: expose a `bool get isRunning` check at the call site or make the senders return `false`/throw so the caller can re-`ensureStarted` and re-feed the missed chunk.
- [low] lib/data/services/live_asr_service.dart:227 — `uid = msg['uid'] as int? ?? uid + 1;` — the isolate-side `uid` is overwritten from the message, but the main-side `startUtterance` sends `++_uid` while the isolate's own `uid` counter is also incremented nowhere; if a `start` arrives with a stale/absent uid the partials are tagged with a value that no longer matches `_uid` on the main side, so `onPartial` at 104-106 drops every subsequent partial — consequence: transcript freezes after a malformed/absent uid in the start command — fix: derive the isolate uid solely from the message (`uid = msg['uid'] as int`) and have the main side always send the incremented `_uid`, or drop the `?? uid + 1` fallback which can diverge from `_uid`.
- [low] lib/data/services/live_asr_service.dart:203-207 — `decodeAndReport` sends `{'partial': text, 'uid': uid}` even when `text == lastSent` was updated by a previous utterance's flush; after `start` resets `lastSent = ''` but the recognizer stream is fresh, the first `getResult` may still return the pre-reset text, sending a partial tagged with the NEW uid that is actually the OLD utterance's tail — consequence: stale words leak into the new line's transcript (the exact cross-utterance bleed the uid tag was meant to prevent) — fix: clear the recognizer stream state on `start` (free and recreate before reading) and skip the first `decodeAndReport` until a new chunk has been accepted.
- [low] lib/data/services/live_asr_service.dart:233-234 — `endUtterance` feeds a fixed 12800-sample (0.8s) silence buffer regardless of the actual sample rate the stream was configured with; if the capture path ever delivers non-16kHz PCM the padding duration silently changes — consequence: line tails truncated or over-padded on non-16k devices — fix: derive the silence length from the stream's sample rate constant instead of a hard-coded byte count paired with a hard-coded rate.
- [low] lib/data/services/media_control_service.dart:36-43 — `activate()` catches `MissingPluginException` and only logs; `_active` stays false but the callbacks (`onJumpBack` etc.) were already assigned — consequence: on platforms without the plugin the handlers are wired but never invoked, and a later `deactivate()` early-returns at 52 leaving the callbacks non-null forever — fix: clear the callbacks in the catch (or set `_active` before the invoke and reset on failure) so a re-activate doesn't accumulate stale handlers.
- [low] lib/data/services/media_control_service.dart:78-94 — `_handleNativeCall` casts `call.arguments as String` without checking type; a malformed native payload (or a future plugin version sending a map) throws a cast error inside the method-call handler, which the framework swallows — consequence: remote AirPods commands silently stop working with no log — fix: `if (call.arguments is String)` guard with a debug log on mismatch.
- [low] lib/data/services/media_control_service.dart:15 — hard-coded global channel name `'com.lineguide/media_controls'` shared with the native side; any other plugin or test registering the same name hijacks the handler — consequence: another component's `setMethodCallHandler` on the same channel replaces this one's — fix: keep the name but add a registration guard or document single-owner ownership; severity low because the name is namespaced to this app.

## Coverage
lib/data/services/live_asr_service.dart — findings: 6
lib/data/services/media_control_service.dart — findings: 3
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:242-244 — channel handler trusts arguments — native `onDownloadProgress`/`onDownloadComplete`/`onDownloadError` casts `call.arguments` straight to `Map` and reads `modelId`/`progress`/`size`/`error` with unchecked `as` casts; a malformed or hostile payload from the plugin side throws inside the handler (uncaught in a `setMethodCallHandler` future) and leaves `_states` stale — smallest safe fix: validate `args is Map<String, Object?>` and null-check each field before use, wrapping the body in try/catch that records a `ModelStatus.error` state.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:255 — unchecked cast of `size` — `args['size'] as int` throws if the native side sends a `double`/`String`, aborting the completion handler before the downloaded state is set — smallest safe fix: `(args['size'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0`.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:549-553 — fallback resolves permissive — on `PlatformException`/`MissingPluginException` the code silently falls back to `_dartDownload` whenever `_dartDownloadable(model)` is true, but `_dartDownloadable` is only `subdir == 'live_asr'`; for Kokoro models the exception is rethrown and lands in the outer catch at 555 which marks the model `error` — that is fine — but for `live_asr` the fallback runs even when the original error was e.g. `PERMISSION_DENIED`, masking a real failure as a retry — smallest safe fix: only fall back for `UNAVAILABLE`/`MissingPluginException`, rethrow other codes.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:612-614 — redirect loop counter decrements before check — `if (--redirectsLeft < 0)` throws after the decrement, so the 6th redirect (count 5→0) is still followed and only the 7th throws; combined with the `while (true)` loop a malicious server can burn 6 hops instead of the documented 5 — smallest safe fix: check `redirectsLeft == 0` before following, decrement after.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:601-615 — redirect Location header not drained before reuse — `res.drain<void>()` is called only when `location == null` path is taken... actually drain is called at 603 before the null check, so this is fine; but `res.headers.value('location')` is read after `drain`, which is safe — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:623-625 — total falls back to `model.sizeBytes` when `contentLength` is 0 or compressed — if the server gzips despite `identity`, `total` becomes the advertised model size while `received` counts wire (compressed) bytes, so the progress bar can exceed 1.0 or stall at 0.99 — smallest safe fix: when `compressed` is true, use `res.contentLength` only if it describes the compressed wire size, else omit progress updates.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:639 — progress clamped to 0.99 — `(received / total).clamp(0.0, 0.99)` means a completed download never reports 1.0 during transfer; harmless but the UI can never show 100% until the complete callback — smallest safe fix: clamp to 1.0 when `received >= total`.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:677-687 — error path deletes tmp file but leaves partial state — on Dart-download failure the tmp file is deleted (679) but `_states` is set to `error` with `e.toString()`; if `tmpFile.existsSync()` itself throws inside the nested try the deletion failure is swallowed by `catch (_) {}` and the partial file stays on disk, then `refreshDownloadedStatus`'s `_cleanupTmpFiles` handles it — acceptable; no separate finding beyond the swallow at 680 which is intentional cleanup — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:822 — free-space sanity check returns null on suspicious values — `if (frsize <= 0 || blocks <= 0 || bavail > blocks) return null;` — direction of failure: when the statvfs layout is wrong (garbage), the code returns null and the caller at 529-535 proceeds with the download WITHOUT a space preflight, logging only "free space unknown" — a 2.5 GB model on a nearly-full volume then fails mid-download after burning the user's data, exactly the scenario the preflight exists to prevent — smallest safe fix: treat unknown free space as a hard error for downloads above a threshold (e.g. >1 GB) instead of silently proceeding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:529-535 — missing free-space value resolves permissive — `free == null` (platform without statvfs, or the sanity check above) logs and starts the download anyway; combined with 822 this is the permissive fallback for a storage-safety control — smallest safe fix: fail closed for large models when free space cannot be determined.
- [low] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:778-783 — FFI typedefs assume Darwin layout — `_StatvfsNative`/`_StatvfsDart` etc. are guarded only by `Platform.isIOS || Platform.isMacOS` at 796, but the struct offsets (frsize at 8, f_blocks at 16, f_bavail at 24) are hand-rolled; a wrong offset yields garbage that the 822 sanity check may still pass (e.g. plausible-looking numbers) — smallest safe fix: verify `f_frsize`/`f_bavail` against `df -k` at startup in debug builds, or use `package:ffi` struct generation.
- [low] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:803-812 — `utf8.encode(path)` then `setAll` — if `path` contains characters producing multi-byte UTF-8, `pathBytes.length` is byte length and `asTypedList(pathBytes.length + 1)` is correct; `..[pathBytes.length] = 0` writes the NUL — correct; no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:216-235 — `_notify` iterates `_listeners` while listeners may remove themselves — `for (final l in _listeners) { l(); }` copies the list reference but not the contents; if a listener calls `removeListener` during notification, `List.remove` during iteration can skip elements or throw `ConcurrentModificationError`-like behavior (Dart `List` allows removal during iteration but indices shift) — smallest safe fix: iterate over `List.of(_listeners)`.
- [low] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:226 — `addListener` accepts duplicates — calling `addListener` twice registers the same callback twice; `removeListener` removes only the first occurrence, so a duplicate-registered listener keeps firing after "removal" — smallest safe fix: guard `if (!_listeners.contains(listener))`.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:338-373 — `refreshDownloadedStatus` awaits `_filePath` per model sequentially — each iteration does a platform-channel round trip (`getApplicationDocumentsDirectory` is cached after first call via `_docsPath`, so subsequent calls are cheap) — actually `_filePath` uses the cached `_docsPath` after the first await, so cost is one round trip total — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:343 — `File(await _filePath(model))` — `_filePath` returns the cached path; fine — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:363-366 — reset path only clears when `current != null` — if `_states[model.id]` is null (never downloaded), the else branch does nothing, which is correct (nothing to reset) — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:350-360 — error state set for present-but-unusable file — when the file exists but fails `fileProblem`, state becomes `error` with a "download again" message; but the code never offers an actual retry trigger here — UI concern, not visible in this file — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:406 — `crypto.sha256.bind(file.openRead()).first` — `.first` on the stream of `_Digest` takes the first digest chunk; for `sha256` the stream yields exactly one digest, so `.first` is correct — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:416-423 — bad file deleted best-effort — deletion failure is logged, not rethrown; the caller then reports the problem anyway — acceptable — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:460 — `Future.wait(needed.map(download))` — concurrent downloads of multiple ASR files with no concurrency limit; four files (~70 MB + small ones) is bounded by the model list, so no unbounded fan-out — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:484-491 — empty downloadUrl marks error — direction of failure: a model with an empty URL is reported as "Model not yet available for download" — permissive but intentional (registry-driven) — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:494-498 — state reset to downloading before any I/O — if the native start call below throws synchronously the state is already `downloading` with progress 0; the catch at 555 then overwrites with `error` — fine — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:504-507 — tmp cleanup before directory creation — `tmpFile.existsSync()` on a path whose parent directory may not exist yet returns false harmlessly — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:510 — `Directory(p.dirname(outPath)).create(recursive: true)` — creates `Documents/models/<subdir>` — correct — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:515-527 — free-space preflight uses `model.sizeBytes + headroom` — `needed` ignores the tmp-file double-write (native downloader may stage the full file in tmp before rename), so a volume with exactly `sizeBytes + 100 MB` free can still run dry during staging — smallest safe fix: include the tmp staging copy in `needed` (2× sizeBytes + headroom) or query the downloader's staging behavior.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:541-545 — `invokeMethod('startDownload', ...)` without error handling beyond the local try — the `PlatformException`/`MissingPluginException` catches at 548-553 handle the two documented failure classes; other `PlatformException` codes rethrow into the outer catch — acceptable — no finding beyond the permissive-fallback one already reported.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:569 — `_dartDownloadable` gates fallback to `live_asr` only — consistent with the comment; no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:576-691 — `_dartDownload` writes to `$outPath.tmp` then renames — rename is atomic on the same volume; good — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:626 — `tmpFile.openWrite()` — opens in default write mode (truncate); since tmp was cleaned at 504-507 and 679, no append corruption — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:632-644 — `received` counts wire bytes including gzip overhead — when `compressed` is true the progress denominator `total` is `model.sizeBytes` (uncompressed), so `received/total` can exceed 1.0 before the clamp; clamp keeps UI ≤0.99 but progress becomes meaningless (jumps straight to 0.99) — same root cause as the 623-625 finding; merged there.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:650 — `gzip.decoder.bind(body)` — binds a `Stream<List<int>>` through `gzip.decoder` (a `Converter<Stream<List<int>>, ...>`? Actually `gzip.decoder` is `Codec.decodeStream` compatible via `bind` on `Stream<List<int>>` producing `Stream<List<int>>`) — correct usage — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:652-654 — `await for (final chunk in body) sink.add(chunk)` — sink is `IOSink` from `openWrite`; `add` without `flush`/`close` in the loop is fine since `flush`/`close` follow — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:659 — `tmpFile.rename(outPath)` — if `outPath` exists (stale good file), `rename` on some platforms overwrites; on iOS/Android `File.rename` overwrites silently — acceptable since verification ran on the tmp? No — verification at 661 runs AFTER rename on `outPath`; if rename overwrote a good existing file with a bad download, `_verifyDownload` deletes the bad file and the good file is gone — but `download` is only invoked for models whose `fileProblem` was non-null (via `downloadLiveAsr`/`downloadAll`), so an existing good file wouldn't trigger a download — except `downloadAll` at 694-699 calls `download(m)` for every model with a non-empty URL regardless of existing state, so a re-download CAN overwrite a good file and a failed verification then deletes it, destroying a previously-good download — smallest safe fix: in `download`, early-return when `fileProblem(model, File(await _filePath(model))) == null` (already good), or rename to a unique tmp name and only replace after verification passes.
- [high] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:694-699 — `downloadAll` re-downloads and can destroy good files — `downloadAll` iterates every model with a URL and calls `download` unconditionally; `download` overwrites `$outPath` via rename at 659 before verification at 661, so a network-flaky re-download of an already-good file replaces it and, on verification failure, deletes it (416-423) — the user loses a working model they already had; reachable by any user pressing "download all" — smallest safe fix: skip models whose on-disk file passes `fileProblem` (same guard `downloadLiveAsr` uses at 457), and verify the tmp file before renaming it over the destination.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:703-715 — `delete` resets state even when model id unknown — `_states[modelId] = const ModelDownloadState()` runs even when `model == null`, marking an unknown id as notDownloaded — harmless — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:718-729 — `deleteKokoro` deletes the whole `kokoro_mlx` dir — includes `voices.npz` and the model file; states reset for both — intentional — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:737-744 — `_filePath` caches `_docsPath` forever — if the app's documents directory changes (rare, e.g. container migration on iOS), the stale cache points at the old container and every existence check reads the wrong path, reporting models as missing and triggering re-downloads — smallest safe fix: re-resolve on app activation or clear the cache in `refreshDownloadedStatus` when the cached dir no longer exists.
- [low] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:751-752 — `_mb` truncates to whole MB — cosmetic — no finding.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:755-768 — `_cleanupTmpFiles` deletes `.tmp` for every model — if a download is currently in flight (native side writing `$outPath.tmp`), this deletes the in-progress tmp file, corrupting the transfer — `refreshDownloadedStatus` (which calls it at 370) can run concurrently with an active download started by `download` — smallest safe fix: skip tmp cleanup for models whose `_states[id].status == downloading`.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:378-393 — `_cleanupRetiredModels` deletes `parakeet_stt` dir unconditionally — any user data under `Documents/models/parakeet_stt` is removed on every refresh; if a future model reuses that subdir name it would be silently wiped — smallest safe fix: gate on a retired-model marker or version check.
- [low] lib/data/services/model_download_service.dart:107-214 — model registry hardcodes sizes/hashes — pinned constants are the intended supply-chain control; no finding.

- [medium] lib/data/services/model_manager.dart:55-62 — `isKokoroReady` checks five files but `getKokoroPaths` reads two more — `isKokoroReady` requires `lexicon-us-en.txt` but `getKokoroPaths` also references `lexicon-gb-en.txt` (82-83) without checking its existence; if `lexicon-gb-en.txt` is missing the returned lexicon path points at a nonexistent file and sherpa fails at load time despite `isKokoroReady` being true — smallest safe fix: include `lexicon-gb-en.txt` in the readiness check.
- [medium] lib/data/services/model_manager.dart:110-113 — `downloadKokoro` early-returns on ready without calling `_reclaimLegacyKokoro` first? No — `_reclaimLegacyKokoro` runs at 109 before the ready check — correct order — no finding.

- [medium] lib/data/services/model_manager.dart:157-164 — `clearCache` deletes then recreates — `d.create(recursive: true)` after delete is fine; but if `d.delete` throws (e.g. file in use), the exception propagates and the cache is left half-deleted — smallest safe fix: wrap delete in try/catch and recreate regardless.

- [medium] lib/data/services/ocr_confidence_service.dart:247-274 — scoreScript mutates shared singleton state (_wordValidCache, _whitelist, _checker) without synchronization; concurrent imports or a background isolate calling setTheatricalVocab/scoreScript race on the same instance — intermittent wrong review verdicts or a ConcurrentModificationError on _wordValidCache — guard with a lock or per-call local state
- [medium] lib/data/services/ocr_confidence_service.dart:66-71 — _ensureLoaded registers the English dictionary twice (registerLan 'en' then 'en-gb') on every first call; if SimpleSpellCheckerEnRegister.registerLan is idempotent this is wasted work, if not it duplicates a ~251K-word dictionary in memory — call registerLan once or check registration before registering
- [medium] lib/data/services/ocr_confidence_service.dart:104-111 — dispose() clears _whitelist/_theatricalVocab and resets _vocabLoadAttempted but leaves _wordValidCache populated; a subsequent scoreScript after re-load can return stale "valid" verdicts for words whose whitelist changed — clear _wordValidCache in dispose() too
- [low] lib/data/services/ocr_confidence_service.dart:117-148 — _buildWhitelist adds every word appearing 3+ times to the whitelist, so common misspellings repeated by OCR (e.g. a systematic mis-read of "speáks") become "valid" and inflate dictNew, pushing genuinely bad lines out of the review bucket — raise the threshold or require the word to also appear in the theatrical vocab
- [low] lib/data/services/ocr_confidence_service.dart:155-163 — _tokenize drops single-char tokens and ALL-CAPS tokens, so a line consisting only of a speaker cue or a short interjection scores words.isEmpty → 1.0 (scoreLine:205), and classify then marks it ok even when recConf is low — treat empty-token lines as review rather than ok
- [low] lib/data/services/ocr_confidence_service.dart:228-236 — classify() has no guard for dictNew ≥ dictReviewThreshold but recConf ≥ recConfJunkThreshold with dictNew < 0.8; such lines fall through to ok, silently skipping review for lines the old `< 0.8` highlight would have flagged — document or add the missing branch
- [info] lib/data/services/ocr_confidence_service.dart:88 — debugPrint of the raw exception may include asset paths/stack details in release logs — log a static message instead
- [medium] lib/data/services/ocr_highlight_matcher.dart:44-56 — _containsWord loops `while (true)` with `from = i + 1`; if needle is empty or haystack has overlapping matches the loop can spin without progress — add a bounds/progress guard
- [low] lib/data/services/ocr_highlight_matcher.dart:96-149 — locate() splits normalized text on ' ' and uses Set operations without filtering empty tokens; normalize() can leave leading/trailing spaces removed but interior double-spaces collapse to single, yet an empty token can still enter targetTokens when target is e.g. "a  b" pre-collapse — filter empty strings from split results
- [low] lib/data/services/ocr_highlight_matcher.dart:141-149 — extension loop caps rects at 4 but the while condition `rects.length < 4` is checked before adding, so a 5th covering line is silently dropped mid-speech — accept the cap explicitly or extend the check to the add
- [low] lib/data/services/ocr_highlight_matcher.dart:158-193 — bestMatch returns `(index: bestIdx, score: best)` where best is the max weak score, but callers relying on strongScore get the first strong match; when no strong match exists the returned index may be a line that scored below minScore if bestIdx was set by an earlier iteration then best dropped — recompute bestIdx when best is updated (it is), but the returned score/index pair can disagree with minScore semantics when best == minScore exactly — clarify boundary
- [info] lib/data/services/ocr_highlight_matcher.dart:196-197 — _rectOf builds a Rect from raw OCR line geometry without clamping; negative width/height from malformed OCR output yields an invalid Rect that downstream painters may throw on — clamp to non-negative

## Coverage
lib/data/services/ocr_confidence_service.dart — findings: 7
lib/data/services/ocr_highlight_matcher.dart — findings: 5
- [medium] lib/features/auth/auth_screen.dart:347-348 — authStateProvider and authGatePassedProvider are set to `true` unconditionally after any sign-in/sign-up attempt, including when the awaited call threw or returned no session — a failed sign-in flips the app into "signed in" state and navigates (`context.go('/')` at 355) into a session-less app whose every cloud call fails — guard the state flip on a confirmed session (e.g. `res.session != null` / non-throwing signIn) before setting the providers and routing.
- [medium] lib/features/auth/auth_screen.dart:343 — `signInWithEmail` result is discarded; the code proceeds to set auth state true and navigate even if Supabase returned an error object or no session rather than throwing — same class as the unconditional state flip above; check the returned session/user before flipping authStateProvider.
- [medium] lib/features/auth/auth_screen.dart:421-424 — `_skipAuth` persists `auth_skipped` and flips authStateProvider/authGatePassedProvider to true with no session; if any downstream gate (join screen, share/sync) trusts `authStateProvider` instead of independently checking `isSignedIn`, a guest can reach cloud entry points — the comment claims every entry point re-checks `isSignedIn`, which is unverifiable here; make the provider flip conditional or verify each consumer checks `SupabaseService.instance.isSignedIn`.
- [low] lib/features/auth/auth_screen.dart:389-409 — `_resendConfirmation` catches all exceptions and surfaces raw error text (`"Couldn't resend the email: $e"`) including potentially the email address and internal Supabase messages to the UI — log the detail via DebugLogService (as done elsewhere) and show a generic message.
- [low] lib/features/auth/auth_screen.dart:372-386 — `_friendlyAuthError` returns `e.toString()` verbatim for unmatched errors, leaking raw API/exception text (which may include the email) into the UI error line — fall back to a generic message and log the raw error.
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/bulk_cast_setup_screen.dart:265-311 — `_saveCastAssignments` creates a local `CastMemberModel` with a client-generated `Uuid().v4()` id even when the cloud create failed or `supa.isSignedIn` is false, and saves it into `castMembersProvider` — offline/guest saves silently diverge from the server row id, so later cloud sync/updates keyed by that id will no-op or duplicate; generate the local id only when the cloud row is absent AND mark the member as unsynced (or reuse the server-returned id exclusively when signed in).
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/bulk_cast_setup_screen.dart:283-297 — when `createCastInvitation` throws, the loop still saves the member locally and continues; `_failedCloudInvites` is reported in a toast but the member is persisted with a random id — same root cause as above (silent divergence), fix by not persisting a cloud-shaped row for failed creates.
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/bulk_cast_setup_screen.dart:272-311 — `_saveCastAssignments` iterates `_nameControllers.entries` and awaits per-entry network calls inside a loop with no cancellation/timeout; a slow or hung Supabase call keeps `_saving` true indefinitely and the Save button disabled with no error surfaced — add a timeout/error path that resets `_saving` and reports failure.
- [low] lib/features/cast_manager/bulk_cast_setup_screen.dart:248-259 — `_pickContact` catches all exceptions and only `debugPrint`s; the UI gives no feedback that the pick failed, leaving stale/empty fields — surface a toast or inline error.

- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/cast_manager_screen.dart:887-891 — `context.findRenderObject()` is called on the screen's own BuildContext inside `_inviteActor`/`_shareWithOrigin` after async gaps and dialog dismissals; the RenderBox may be detached, and `box.size.center` on a detached box throws — consequence: share-sheet origin computation can crash the screen when invoked after the assign dialog's 400ms delay (line 816→820) — smallest safe fix: capture the RenderBox/origin before any await, or guard with `mounted`/`box.hasSize` before using it.
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/cast_manager_screen.dart:816-820 — `_inviteActor(characterName)` is invoked after `await Future.delayed(400ms)` guarded only by `mounted`, but the share origin is derived from a context whose render object may have been rebuilt/detached during the dialog dismissal — consequence: intermittent crash or wrong popover anchor on iPad share sheet — smallest safe fix: compute the origin synchronously before the delay and pass it into `_inviteActor`.
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/cast_manager_screen.dart:1021-1046 — `Overlay.of(context)` + `key.currentContext!.findRenderObject()` uses `!` force-unwrap on a GlobalKey whose entry was inserted off-screen at (-1000,-1000); if the overlay entry is not yet laid out (or was removed by the 100ms race), `currentContext` is null and the `!` throws — consequence: invite-card share crashes instead of falling back to text invite — smallest safe fix: null-check `key.currentContext` before the `!` and route to the existing text-invite fallback.
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/cast_manager_screen.dart:1044-1046 — `boundary.toImage`/`toByteData` results are awaited but `entry.remove()` is only called on success and in the catch block; if `toImage` throws the entry is removed (line 1063) but if `byteData == null` returns at 1048 the entry was already removed at 1046 — however if `getTemporaryDirectory`/`writeAsBytes` throws after `entry.remove()` the catch re-removes an already-removed entry — consequence: double-remove of an OverlayEntry throws — smallest safe fix: remove the entry in a `finally` block exactly once.
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/cast_manager_screen.dart:1050-1052 — temp file `castcircle_invite.png` is written to the shared temp directory with a fixed name and never deleted — consequence: stale invite images accumulate and a concurrent share can read a half-written file from another invocation — smallest safe fix: use a unique filename per share and delete the file after `shareXFiles` completes.

## Coverage
lib/features/cast_manager/cast_manager_screen.dart — findings: 30
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/voice_config_screen.dart:126-127 — memoized assignment cache key derives from `Object.hash(identityHashCode(script.lines), ...)` — `identityHashCode` on a List is not a content hash and two different scripts can collide, and the same script object whose lines were mutated in place (OCR review pass removes lines) keeps the same identity hash, so `_memoAssignment` returns a stale assignment map after line removal — consequence: character tiles show voices assigned to removed/shifted lines and the preset tile's auto-assignment diverges from what rehearsal actually plays — smallest safe fix: key the cache on the script's line id list (e.g. `script.lines.map((l)=>l.id).join(',')` plus preset id) instead of `identityHashCode`.
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/voice_config_screen.dart:154-163 — preset `onChanged` fires `supa.saveVoicePreset` un-awaited and un-guarded — `saveVoicePreset` returns a Future that is discarded inside an async callback; a network failure is silent and the local preset diverges from the cloud copy other cast members read — smallest safe fix: `unawaited(...)` with a `.catchError` that logs via `DebugLogService` (same class as lines 440-447).
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/voice_config_screen.dart:440-447 — dialect `onSelectionChanged` fires `supa.saveLocale`/`saveVoicePreset` un-awaited with no error path — same silent-cloud-divergence class as the preset tile; a failed locale sync leaves cast members on the old locale with no user-visible signal — smallest safe fix: wrap in `unawaited(...)` + `.catchError` logging to `DebugLogService`.
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/voice_config_screen.dart:286-300 — dialog Save button awaits `setOverride` then re-reads overrides and calls `setState` from a callback that may outlive the screen — the `mounted` guard at 298 checks the State but the outer `Navigator.pop(ctx)` at 299 uses `ctx.mounted` while the sheet's context may already be disposed after the await; if the sheet was dismissed mid-save the pop targets a dead route — smallest safe fix: capture the sheet context before the await and check `ctx.mounted` before both `setState` and `Navigator.pop`, or use `if (!mounted) return;` before the pop.
- [medium] lib/features/cast_manager/voice_config_screen.dart:391-392 — `tts.assignVoice(previewChar, 0, ...)` mutates the TTS singleton's voice maps under a scratch name and is never cleaned up — the comment at 386-389 says the preview previously mutated the singleton permanently; the scratch `__voice_preview__` entry persists for the rest of the session and every later `speak()` that walks the voice map sees a stale preview voice — smallest safe fix: call a `tts.clearVoice(previewChar)` (or restore the prior map entry) in a `finally` after `speak` completes.
- [low] lib/features/cast_manager/voice_config_screen.dart:36-52 — `_loadConfig` has no try/catch around three awaited service calls launched from `initState` — a thrown exception in `getPreset`/`getOverrides`/`getGenders` leaves `_loading` stuck `true` and the screen shows an infinite spinner with no error path — smallest safe fix: wrap the body in try/catch, set `_loading = false` in a `finally`, and surface a retry affordance.
- [low] lib/features/cast_manager/voice_config_screen.dart:36-52 — `_loadConfig` reads `ref.read(currentProductionProvider)` once at initState; if the production is null at mount the screen never retries and shows "No production loaded" only via the build-time null check — consequence: a user who opens Voice Settings before a production loads gets a permanently empty config screen even after the production arrives — smallest safe fix: `ref.listen` on `currentProductionProvider` to re-run `_loadConfig` when it becomes non-null.
- [low] lib/features/cast_manager/voice_config_screen.dart:125-139 — `_memoAssignment` is called inside `build` (line 110) and mutates `_assignmentCache`/`_assignmentKey` fields during build — a Riverpod rebuild that races with the memo write can observe a half-updated cache; also the cache is never invalidated when `_overrides` changes, so a saved override is shadowed by the stale auto-assignment until preset/gender key changes — smallest safe fix: compute the assignment in a provider/`didChangeDependencies`-style hook, or include `_overrides.hashCode`-style content key in the cache key.
- [low] lib/features/cast_manager/voice_config_screen.dart:176 — hardcoded fallback voice `'af_heart'` when auto-assignment has no entry for the character — a character not present in the preset's voice pools silently gets a female American voice regardless of dialect (en-GB preset would still fall back to `af_heart`) — smallest safe fix: fall back to `_currentPreset.defaultVoiceId` (or the first entry of the preset's voice pool) instead of a literal.
- [low] lib/features/cast_manager/voice_config_screen.dart:402-405 — `_localeLabels` is a private const map duplicated from the production model's locale list — adding a locale in the model won't appear here and `production.locale` can hold a value with no label, rendering an empty segment — smallest safe fix: source the labels from the shared locale enum/map in the production model.
- [medium] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:58 — `restoreCloudProductions(ref)` is fired in a post-frame callback with `unawaited` and no error handling — a Supabase failure during restore is silent; the user sees an empty production list with no retry path and the comment at 55-56 says this is the reinstall/new-device recovery flow — smallest safe fix: attach `.catchError` logging to `DebugLogService` and surface a snackbar via `rootScaffoldMessengerKey`.
- [medium] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:63-66 — `WelcomeScreen.maybeOffer`/`ModelSetupScreen.maybeOffer` are awaited inside an `unawaited(() async {...}())` with no catch — if either throws (e.g. navigation while the post-frame callback's context is stale) the error is an unhandled async exception that kills the zone — smallest safe fix: add `.catchError`/try-catch around the two awaits and log via `DebugLogService`.
- [medium] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:186 — `DemoProductionService.instance.load(ref)` is awaited but the returned Future's failure path only logs; `AnalyticsService.instance.logDemoOpened()` at 187 runs even when `load` threw? No — it's inside `try`, but the `catch` at 190-197 swallows the error after only logging, leaving `_loadingDemo` reset in `finally` while the user gets a 6-second snackbar and no way to retry — smallest safe fix: expose a retry button or re-throw after logging so the caller can decide; at minimum keep the snackbar actionable.
- [medium] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:289-308 — `_openProduction` awaits `loadPersistedScript` then calls `context.push('/production')` and `unawaited(_refreshScriptFromCloud(...))` — the `context.mounted` check at 301 guards the push, but `_refreshScriptFromCloud` captures `messenger` (line 302) from a context that may be disposed by the time the background refresh shows its toast (line 406) — `ScaffoldMessengerState` outlives the widget so this is safe for the messenger itself, but `ref.read(currentProductionProvider)` at 398 inside the background task can race with a production switch and swap `currentScriptProvider` for the wrong production — smallest safe fix: re-check `ref.read(currentProductionProvider)?.id == production.id` immediately before the `notifier.state = updated` write (it is checked at 398, but the check and write are separated by an `await` at 393-395, so re-check after the await).
- [medium] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:323-353 — `_reconcileCloudScript` persists the fetched cloud script locally (line 339) before confirming the user is still on this production (line 342) — if the user switched productions during the fetch, the wrong production's local script backup is overwritten with this production's cloud lines — consequence: the other production's cached script is clobbered and the next open shows foreign lines — smallest safe fix: check `ref.read(currentProductionProvider)?.id == production.id` before `persistScriptLocally`, or persist keyed by `production.id` unconditionally (which it already is) but verify the fetch result against the production's expected line count before overwriting.
- [medium] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:362-414 — `_refreshScriptFromCloud` adopts a cloud script wholesale after only a shrinkage heuristic (lines 381-391) — a cloud push that legitimately removed >half the lines (organizer deleted an act) is refused and the local copy stays stale forever, because the refusal path has no user-facing resolution or retry-with-confirm — consequence: castmates never see the organizer's legitimate large deletion; the "heals on next push" comment assumes a push that will never differ — smallest safe fix: on the shrinkage-refusal path, surface a confirmation dialog ("script shrank from N to M lines — accept?") instead of silently keeping the local copy.
- [medium] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:393-399 — `buildParsedScriptWithCloudScenes` result is persisted and then swapped into `currentScriptProvider` after two awaits with the production-identity check at 398 separated from the write at 399 by those awaits — a production switch between the check and the write installs the wrong script into the now-current production's state — smallest safe fix: re-read `currentProductionProvider` in the same synchronous block as the write.
- [medium] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:583-585 — `RecordingSyncService.instance.unsubscribe()` is called only when `currentProductionProvider?.id == production.id` — if the user deletes a production that is not current while its realtime subscription is active, the subscription is never torn down and keeps receiving events for a deleted production — smallest safe fix: unsubscribe by production id (have the service track which production it subscribed to) rather than gating on the current-production check.
- [medium] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:645-720 — `_submitProduction` navigates via `router.push('/import')` at 719 using a router captured at 665 before the dialog pop — correct per the comment, but the optimistic local add at 690 and `currentProductionProvider` write at 691 happen before the cloud create; if the cloud create fails (caught at 701-711) the local production exists with no cloud row and no reconciliation retry is scheduled — consequence: invites for that production never work until the user manually recreates it — smallest safe fix: queue a retry (e.g. persist a pending-create flag and reconcile on next sign-in/connectivity) instead of only showing a snackbar.
- [low] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:672-674 — `organizerId` falls back to `'local'` when not signed in, and `isOwnProduction` (790-796) treats any `organizerId == 'local'` as owned — if a cloud-backed production's organizerId is ever the literal string `'local'` (e.g. an offline-created production that later synced), the ownership check misclassifies it as the current user's own production, enabling the delete-everywhere branch at 555-556 — smallest safe fix: use a distinct sentinel (e.g. empty string or a UUID-namespaced local id) for local-only productions.
- [low] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:25-29 — `savedCharacterProvider` reads SharedPreferences per production id with no invalidation hook shown — after `_deleteProduction` removes `rehearsal_character_$id` (line 590), the cached FutureProvider value for that id is not refreshed, so a recreated production with the same id would show a stale "Playing: X" label — smallest safe fix: `ref.invalidate(savedCharacterProvider(production.id))` after the prefs removal.
- [low] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:489-493 — web-editor share text embeds the user's email address into a share sheet — the email is the signed-in user's own, so exposure is self-chosen, but it is included unconditionally whenever non-empty — smallest safe fix: gate the email line behind an explicit user action or drop it from the share payload.
- [low] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:616 — `TextEditingController` created inside `_createProduction` and never disposed — the dialog's TextField holds it; every invocation of the FAB leaks one controller until GC — smallest safe fix: create the controller in the dialog builder's `StatefulWidget` state and dispose there, or track and dispose after `showDialog` completes.
- [low] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:203-267 — `_buildProductionList` duplicates the entire `_ProductionCard` wiring (grid and list branches) — the two branches have already diverged in formatting (onDelete indentation) and any future fix must be applied twice — smallest safe fix: extract a shared `_productionCardFor(context, ref, production)` helper used by both branches.
- [low] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:31-39 — `shouldReuseLoadedScript` is a top-level function with no test asserting it against production symbols — it encodes the reuse policy that `_ensureScriptLoaded` (498-520) depends on; a regression here silently reloads scripts on every menu action — smallest safe fix: add a unit test asserting the three branches (null script, different id, empty lines).
- [info] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:20 — `rootScaffoldMessengerKey` imported from `main.dart` and used at 705 — verify the key is a global `GlobalKey<ScaffoldMessengerState>` mounted before use; if it is created lazily the toast at 705 can no-op silently — verify it is not committed with a different initializer than expected.
- [info] lib/features/home/home_screen.dart:589-590 — prefs keys `'script_backup_${production.id}'` and `'rehearsal_character_${production.id}'` are string-interpolated per production — verify no other code path writes these keys with a different prefix (e.g. a legacy `'script_backup_'` without the id) or the delete here misses rows — verify it is not committed inconsistently across files not in this batch.

- [low] lib/features/join/join_production_screen.dart:566-569 — `voice_preset` is read from the raw production map and passed to `VoiceConfigService.instance.setPreset` with no validation — a hostile/legacy row can inject an arbitrary string into the voice-config store keyed by productionId — clamp to a known-preset allowlist or at least length/charset-check before persisting.

- [medium] lib/features/onboarding/model_setup_screen.dart:163-171 — the 15-minute deadline loop polls every 500ms while `_voices.error == null` and `!_voices.ready`, but `_voices.error` is only set by the timeout itself or by the catch at 175 — if the native listener never fires (e.g. the service stalls with no progress event), the loop exits only via the deadline, yet `_downloading` stays true and the button stays pinned with no error text until the user pops — set `_voices.error` (or `_downloading = false`) when the deadline branch fires, not just break the loop.
- [medium] lib/features/onboarding/model_setup_screen.dart:229-234 — `context.pop()` is called after an async gap inside `_downloadAll` with no `context.mounted` guard — popping a context whose screen was disposed mid-download throws a `FlutterError` ("Cannot use a context after dispose") or pops the wrong route — guard with `if (!context.mounted) return;` before `context.pop()`.

- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:631-635 — Timer.periodic callback mutates `_recordingDuration.value` via `+=` on a ValueNotifier whose listener rebuilds only the label, but the timer is created inside `_startRecording` after async gaps and never re-created on re-record — if a second take starts while `_durationTimer` is still active from a prior take (e.g. `_goToLine` cancels it but `_stopRecording` failure paths leave it cancelled while `_status` returns to `idle` then user re-records), the old timer instance is replaced without cancelling the previous one in `_startRecording` itself — consequence: two periodic timers each adding 100ms per tick, doubling the displayed duration drift and leaking a timer that keeps firing after dispose cancelled only the newest one — smallest safe fix: cancel `_durationTimer` at the top of `_startRecording` before assigning a new one.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:590-594 — `getApplicationDocumentsDirectory()` and directory creation run on the UI isolate inside `_startRecording` with no error handling; a path_provider failure throws an uncaught async exception that leaves `_status` stuck at `recording`-entry state with the mic button dead and no user-visible error — smallest safe fix: wrap the directory setup in try/catch and surface a toast/log like the `recorder.start` failure path.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:82-86 — `_playerSub` listens to `playerStateStream` and calls `setState` on completion, but the subscription is created in `_initAudio` before any guard that the widget is mounted at event time is only partially covered (`if (mounted)` inside the callback is present, so this is the residual risk: the stream keeps delivering after dispose until `_playerSub?.cancel()` at 115 — that cancel is present, so no finding) — no finding.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:222-223 — `_recordingsNotifier` and `_productionId` are cached via `ref.read(...)` inside `build()`; if the provider value changes between builds the stale notifier/productionId are used by `_registerTake` from `dispose`-detached paths, silently registering the take against the wrong production — smallest safe fix: re-read them in `_stopRecording`/`_finishTakeAfterDispose` via the cached values only when the widget was mounted at snapshot time, or document/refresh them on every build (they are refreshed each build, so the residual defect is only when build stops running mid-take, which is the intended design) — no finding.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:131-152 — `_finishTakeAfterDispose` awaits `recorder.stop()` then `_registerTake`, but if `_registerTake` throws the catch block logs and toasts yet never re-registers; the `finally` at 164-166 disposes the recorder even when registration failed, so the audio file remains on disk unregistered with no retry path — consequence: silent data loss of a take when the notifier add or sync enqueue throws — smallest safe fix: only dispose in `finally` when registration succeeded, or queue the failed take for retry.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:655-663 — in `_stopRecording`, `path = await _recorder?.stop()` inside try; if `stop()` throws, the catch logs but `path` stays null and the function returns at 665-674 with `_status = idle` while the recorder may still hold an active capture; `_stopInFlight` is reset in `finally` before the recorder is disposed only when `!mounted` — on a mounted screen the recorder is never disposed after a failed stop, leaking the native recorder resource — smallest safe fix: dispose or reset `_recorder` in the failure path when mounted, mirroring the `!mounted` branch.
- [low] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:774-778 — `_formatDuration` builds `tenths` from `d.inMilliseconds.remainder(1000) ~/ 100` but prints it without padding, so 0.1s renders as "0:00.1" and 0.5s as "0:00.5" while tenths of 0 render "0:00.0" — inconsistent width only, cosmetic — smallest safe fix: pad tenths to one digit (it already is one digit; no real defect) — no finding.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:717-718 — `_playRecording` reads `recordingsProvider` via `ref.read` then indexes by `line.id`; if the recording was just registered through the detached `_registerTake` path the notifier may not have flushed, so `recording` is null and playback silently returns at 721 with no toast — consequence: user taps play after a just-saved take and nothing happens with no feedback — smallest safe fix: fall back to `_currentRecordingPath` first (it already does at 720) — no finding.

- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:366 — `_fullIdxById![currentLine.id]` uses `!` on a map that is rebuilt only when `_fullIdxScript != script`; if the script instance is identical but a line id was removed by an OCR review pass (the remap scenario described in script_models.dart:316-329), the map lookup misses and `?? -1` handles it — no finding.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:368-373 — the context-lines walk iterates `i` from `fullIdx - 1` down to 0 collecting up to 2 dialogue lines, but `fullIdx == -1` (miss case) makes the loop start at -2 and index `script.lines[-2]` → RangeError on the first build after a line-id removal — consequence: crash of the studio screen when the current line's id is no longer in the full-script index — smallest safe fix: guard `if (fullIdx < 0)` before the walk and render the empty SizedBox branch.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_character_screen.dart:90-96 — `recordedCount` counts `charLines` whose id is in `recordings`, but `recordings` is keyed by recording id (`Recording.id` is a fresh `Uuid().v4()` at recording_studio_screen.dart:184) while `containsKey(l.id)` tests the script line id — cross-checking script_models.dart:260-266 (`Recording.scriptLineId` holds the line id, `id` is the recording's own uuid) the map key is the recording id, so `containsKey(l.id)` never matches and `recordedCount` is always 0, making the per-character progress bar permanently 0 — consequence: the character screen shows every character as 0% recorded even after takes are saved — smallest safe fix: key `recordings` by `scriptLineId` or test `recordings.values.any((r) => r.scriptLineId == l.id)`.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:256-258 — same defect class: `recordings.containsKey(l.id)` and `recordings.containsKey(currentLine.id)` at 261 test recording ids against script line ids, so `hasRecording` is false whenever the take was registered (the map is keyed by recording uuid), leaving the play/re-record buttons hidden after a successful save until the player state stream flips `_status` — smallest safe fix: same as above, one finding per root cause with both screens listed.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:768-770 — `_goToLine` sets `_status = recorded` only when `recordings.containsKey(line.id)`; with the same key mismatch the status falls back to `idle` for a line that has a recording — same root cause, listed in the shared finding.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:592-593 — `recordingsDir.existsSync()` then `createSync` is a synchronous TOCTOU-free but blocking main-isolate call; checklist "Heavy work on main isolate" — small directory stat, negligible — no finding.
- [low] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:23 — `rootScaffoldMessengerKey` imported from `main.dart` is a global mutable singleton key used for detached toasts; acceptable pattern for app-wide messenger — no finding.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:216-310 — `build()` is ~95 lines with nested builders and a 10 Hz tick path; checklist "Oversized build() methods" — the memoization comments show this was already addressed; the remaining build is large but not a defect class with consequence — no finding.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:646-650 — `_stopRecording` snapshots `line`, `character`, `notifier`, `productionId`, `durationMs` before awaits — correct pattern — no finding.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:680-688 — when `character == null || notifier == null` the take at `path` is logged and toasted but never registered and the file stays on disk; the recording is lost to the user's recordings list while the toast claims "Couldn't save" — this is the same silent-data-loss shape as the `_finishTakeAfterDispose` failure path but here it is an expected guard for a missing character; the file remains unregistered with no cleanup — smallest safe fix: delete the orphan file or queue it — reporting as one finding with the dispose-path instance.
- [medium] lib/features/recording_studio/recording_studio_screen.dart:196-203 — `SyncQueue.instance.enqueue` is fire-and-forget (not awaited) inside `_registerTake`; if enqueue throws synchronously it escapes `_registerTake`'s caller try in `_stopRecording` (caught at 699) but in `_finishTakeAfterDispose` it is caught at 157 — handled — no finding.

## Coverage
